title: Sign Library
description: Every standard sign, drawn clean at the same size, grouped by MUTCD type, with California status, sizes, and governing sections.
tableOfContents: false

<div class="srcbar">Federal sign artwork and dimensions: <a href="https://mutcd.fhwa.dot.gov/kno-shs_2024-release-status/index.htm">FHWA Standard Highway Signs, 2024 Edition, Releases 1–6</a>. California-only signs (codes ending in (CA)): cut from the CA MUTCD 2026 figures; search "(CA)" to list them. 1,472 distinct sign codes; 28 federal signs are crossed out (not adopted) in California.</div>

<div class="sseries" data-cat="guide"><h3 id="series-sg60">SG60 &middot; Other</h3><div class="sgrid"><a class="scard" href="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/sg60aca/" data-code="SG60A(CA)" data-name="the post office guide sign sg60a(ca) the post office post office post office guide sign other" data-cat="guide" data-ca="ca"><span class="simg"><img loading="lazy" src="/SoCal-Super-Standards/signs/ca/sg60aca.webp" alt="SG60A(CA) The POST OFFICE"></span><span class="scode">SG60A(CA)</span><span class="sname">The POST OFFICE guide sign</span></a></div></div>

<script>
(() => {
  const q = document.getElementById('sq'), ca = document.getElementById('sca'), cnt = document.getElementById('scount');
  let cat = 'all';
  const alnum = s => s.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '');
  const cards = [...document.querySelectorAll('.scard')];
  cards.forEach(c => { c._c = c.dataset.code.toLowerCase(); c._ca = alnum(c.dataset.code); c._n = c.dataset.name; });
  function run() {
    const raw = q.value.trim().toLowerCase();
    const words = raw.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
    let n = 0;
    cards.forEach(c => {
      const ok = (cat === 'all' || c.dataset.cat === cat)
        && (!ca.checked || !['not-adopted','unverified'].includes(c.dataset.ca))
        && (!words.length || words.every(w => c._c.includes(w) || c._n.includes(w) || (alnum(w).length >= 2 && c._ca.includes(alnum(w)))));
      c.hidden = !ok; if (ok) n++;
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('.sseries').forEach(s => s.hidden = !s.querySelector('.scard:not([hidden])'));
    document.querySelectorAll('.sgroup').forEach(h => {
      let el = h.nextElementSibling, any = false;
      while (el && !el.classList.contains('sgroup')) { if (el.classList.contains('sseries') && !el.hidden) any = true; el = el.nextElementSibling; }
      h.hidden = !any;
    });
    cnt.textContent = n + ' of ' + cards.length + ' signs';
    const u = new URLSearchParams();
    if (q.value.trim()) u.set('q', q.value.trim());
    if (cat !== 'all') u.set('cat', cat);
    if (ca.checked) u.set('ca', '1');
    history.replaceState(null, '', location.pathname + (u.toString() ? '?' + u : '') + location.hash);
  }
  q.addEventListener('input', run); ca.addEventListener('change', run);
  document.querySelectorAll('.sbtns button').forEach(b => b.addEventListener('click', () => {
    document.querySelectorAll('.sbtns button').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('on')); b.classList.add('on'); cat = b.dataset.f; run();
  }));
  const p = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
  if (p.get('q')) q.value = p.get('q');
  if (p.get('ca') === '1') ca.checked = true;
  if (p.get('cat')) {
    const btn = [...document.querySelectorAll('.sbtns button')].find(b => b.dataset.f === p.get('cat'));
    if (btn) { document.querySelectorAll('.sbtns button').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('on')); btn.classList.add('on'); cat = btn.dataset.f; }
  }
  run();
})();
</script>
